- gr1.py (old scratchpad 604f8420): resync (20 rejects -> h snaps to ToF + Alt_KfReset, StabilizerTask.c:1779) steps h median 8 cm (max 18); 31/16/11 resyncs on FD/CF/KF ladder flights, ~all at 0.4 m; drops with resync +-0.3 s: 9/23 FD, 8/13 CF, 2/15 KF.
- gr3.py open-loop replay (raw cm*cos tilt, band 5-500 cm, hold only). Score: snaps = h step >3 cm in 10 ms per min; slow = rms(h - 1 s median of raw) cm; take = share of a >=5 cm one-update raw jump absorbed in 0.1 s.
  flown-like (jump gate+resync, R 1 cm): snaps 20/18.7/12.7, slow 3.3/3.0/3.0, take 0.12/0/0.10 (logged alt_cf_h snaps 41.6/19.4/20.0 -> replay is optimistic).
  no gate R 2 cm: snaps 0/0/0, slow 3.1/2.4/2.4, take 0.45/0.39/0.45. No gate R 3 cm: 0/0/0, 3.1/2.4/2.3, take 0.41/0.32/0.40.
  gate 3 sigma R 3 cm (0.5 s timeout reset): snaps 5/3/0, slow 4.3/3.6/2.3, take 0.35/0.29/0.31. +median3: snaps 3.3/1.5/0, take 0.23/0.25/0.26. gate 4 R2: 3.3/1.5/0, slow 3.5/2.5/2.5. timeout 1 s worse (slow 7.7).
  READ: snaps come from HARD steps (resync, timeout reset), not from fusing. Honest R (2-3 cm) alone removes them; a gate adds rejects -> resets -> snaps unless the reset is soft. Trade: no-gate absorbs ~40% of a phantom jump in 0.1 s; gate3+med3 ~25%.
- PROPOSED firmware candidate (replay only, not flown): KF R 3 cm, median-3 prefilter, innovation gate 3-4 sigma, and replace the hard resync/reset with a soft re-acquire (inflate P00 instead of snapping h). Must be scored closed-loop before adoption; replay of the soft re-acquire not yet done.
- NEXT: (a) replay soft re-acquire (P00 inflate on timeout) in gr3.py; (b) NEXT 3 camera path grep g_ext_pos (API/ekf_of.c, StabilizerTask.c) + mode-4 prototype; (c) NEXT 4 open-items rows (ToF jumps/resync snaps at 0.4 m, 20 s hold test, deck log plan missing ToF/alt_cf); add gr results to docs/lab/z-filter-research.md.

# CEO state - 2026-10-24 07:40 - design settled for refusing_write
Runner (campaign_runner.py:425-463): ground writes run BEFORE arm/idle; send()==False -> AbortDecision level 1
"takeoff refused at <label>: <last_error>". So kind "refuse": check_write accepts; write_label = "REFUSED <field>: <reason>";
send_write returns False WITHOUT sending (no raise: a raise would escape the runner). layer_write also refuses a
field >= 32 on axis 0 (no idx encoding until fw item 1 wire format). Parse sites: scenario_schema.py:375, fly_file.py:187.

## 2026-10-21 ~14:20 reflash research, operator said "lets start"
- Operator: the 55 KB/s figure is STALE; measured actual = 92 KB/s = the 921600 ceiling -> serial link IS the bottleneck.
- Found: ESP32-C5 Series Datasheet v1.5 "programmable baud rates up to 5 MBaud" (UART). DroneBridge releases v2.3.0-v2.4.1
  name no C5; v2.2.0 RC1: "ESP32-C5 Support WIP - Not with this release since ESP-NOW library has issues compiling".
  esptool docs give no get-security-info field list (need a live run).
- Installed esptool 5.6.0 (PyPI, operator "lets start"). flash-id + get-security-info on COM9: "No serial data received"
  = CH340 DTR/RTS NOT wired to EN/IO0 -> no auto-reset; needs BOOT button held at power-up, then `--before no-reset`.
  Nothing read or written. Module verified alive after: +VER:1.0.6, +BAUD:921600.
- NEXT: operator: does the module have a BOOT button? Then: get-security-info, flash-id, `read-flash 0 ALL` x2 + sha256
  compare (backup). Then decide custom ESP-IDF UART->UDP bridge (no DroneBridge C5 build) + FC USART3 BRR 21 (FC flash).
  Read-only log: scratchpad/esptool_ro.log.
- ~14:35 operator: 2 buttons, "RST" + unmarked one near USB-C (likely BOOT, UNVERIFIED). Told: hold unmarked, tap RST,
  release, type "boot" -> then `esptool --before no-reset` get-security-info / flash-id / read-flash x2.
- Operator asked "why not 5 Mbaud?" Answer: baud = FC<->module wire, not radio range. FC USART3 APB1 42 MHz:
  OVER8 max 5.25M; exact rates 2.0 (OVER16 BRR 21), 2.625, 3.0, 3.5, 4.2, 5.25 (OVER8); 5.0 not reachable (5.25 = +5%,
  and above C5's "up to 5 MBaud"). No flow control -> ESP RX buffer overrun on WiFi stalls. Plan: ladder 2 -> 3 -> 4.2,
  measure KB/s + loss each, stop when KB/s stops rising (= producer's real demand). >2M needs OVER8 in the FC driver.
- C5 firmware options found: ESP-AT (Espressif official, has esp32c5 docs, passthrough mode AT+CIPMODE=1, example
  AT+UART_CUR=3000000, ">= ~5 Mbps use SPI/SDIO"); DroneBridge (C5 WIP); custom ESP-IDF bridge; Arduino (C5 version
  unconfirmed). ESP-IDF C5 WiFi doc: "30 MBit/s UDP throughput over the air". KEY UNKNOWN for all: MicoAir GPIO pin map
  (which C5 pins go to the FC UART). Still to research: AT+SAVETRANSLINK (auto passthrough at boot) for C5, pin map source.
- ~14:50 operator did BOOT+RST: LEDs off (expected, ROM loader). `esptool --before no-reset --after no-reset
  get-security-info` rc=0 (log scratchpad/esp_sec.log): Secure Boot Disabled, FLASH ENCRYPTION ENABLED,
  SPI_BOOT_CRYPT_CNT 0x7 (permanent), BLOCK_KEY0 XTS_AES_128_KEY, SECURE DOWNLOAD MODE (stub disabled), JTAG perm.
  disabled. Per IDF C5 flash-encryption doc: release mode = UART can't encrypt; plaintext flash "will soft-brick";
  Secure Download Mode allows only SPI config/baud/basic write/security info -> read-flash blocked -> NO BACKUP
  POSSIBLE. VERDICT: reflash of the MicoAir module is OFF (one-way door, no revert). No read-flash/flash-id/writes run.

- 03:45 D3 FINDING (code, StabilizerTask.c:2243-2275): with kind!=0, locxsPID.Des = PID position loop out
  (locxPID.U) + vx (+ preset vff) is still computed; ADRC/LQR/MPC overwrite locxsPID.U (accel) from position,
  ignoring that vel setpoint. MRAC r_x = locxsPID.Des (mrac.c:2029) -> ref model (1st order bw 2, fit of PID
  FB->Des) follows the PID's velocity demand, not the ADRC/LQR/MPC closed loop. So MRAC injects u_ad to pull the
  drone toward the PID's velocity profile = second controller fighting the nominal. Standard (Lavretsky-Wise
  baseline+adaptive augmentation): ref model must be the nominal controller's closed loop on the nominal plant.
  Regressor xy (mrac.c:354-375): phi=[1, v, v tanh v, u_nom] (INCLUDE_CONTROL_IN_REGRESSOR else-branch, no cross).
  PID gains pid.c:36-38: locx Kp .3214 Ki .0001 Kd 6.29; locxs Kp 3.2242 Ki .0157 Kd 4.73.

## 10-22 ~10:15 research file arrived (docs/research/estimation_related_research.md, operator's, untracked, DO NOT commit)
- Read all 257 lines. Takeaways: S1 Larsen 1998 extrapolation / PX4 EKF2 delayed-fusion buffer; Mahalanobis gate at capture time
  with delay-compensated P; rate gain stops at camera fps + correlated noise (Kurtz&Lin 2019). S2 marker centre = mean of 4 corners,
  subpix weak on ArUco (-10%), 1-euro filter display only (not before EKF). S3 lag vs delay: excite ABOVE lag corner; |G| rolloff pins
  lag, phase slope pins delay; CIFER coherence-weighted cost (w_ap 0.01745); Grauer orthogonal multisine after mixer, |r_ij|<0.9;
  typical tau 17-29 ms + motor lag 20-57 ms. S4 Nogueira Phi (Poor<0.40, Excellent>0.75, one-sided z test) works with varying k
  -> use instead of Jaccard; Meinshausen pi_thr 0.6-0.9. S5 shadow->active: Lipschitz bound (spectral norm), damping -lambda*a,
  slope~1 shadow check (Saj 2026); ground effect T/(1-mu(D/8z)^2), baro dip under rotor wash.
- NEXT: code lookups a-d, then reply (verdict per section + 1 decision Q + 1 rigor Q), then deck rows.
- LOOKUPS DONE (10:25): (d) yellow = trail polyline (0,215,255) of the DRONE tracker uv (live_tracker.py:837), NOT ArUco.
  uv = CoralTracker (median of coral guard-ring pixels in ROI, minus learned `static` mask, live_tracker.py:117) or
  RingTracker (red HSV mean-shift r=150 px, :54-107). Corner latch = partial ring visible (glare/static mask eats one side)
